# Reference evidence register

These IDs distinguish historical source snapshots from fresh, bounded browser observations. A fresh browser observation does not imply database/source access beyond its stated boundary. Never store DMS production row values, credentials, or session material in this register.

| ID | Evidence source | Observation and boundary |
|---|---|---|
| E-DASH-01 | Read-only DMS `routes/dashboard.php`, `Dashboard\{Main,Stats,Isp,Mailer}Controller`, `DashboardAction`, and `resources/views/dashboard/{index,stats,isp,mailer}.blade.php`; AlphaLink dashboard/tracking controllers, report views, campaign/drop event and list-history models; `WorkspaceAnalyticsDashboardTest`, `CampaignTrackingTest`; authenticated AlphaLink 8766 browser reload; 2026-10-26 | DMS source defines Main, All Stats, ISP Stats and Mailer Revenue semantics; no DMS data-bearing page or mutation was used. AlphaLink renders all four routes in the DMS admin shell. Main uses stored campaign open/click/unsubscribe/bot events, Drop visitor events and latest list snapshots; report routes filter by date and ISP/grouping, display an empty state, and cap rows at 50 per page. `WorkspaceAnalyticsDashboardTest`: 6 / 52; `CampaignTrackingTest`: 5 / 43; `DmsAudienceListHistoryTest`: 5 / 38 (combined 16 / 133; E-TRACK-05). Reloading the existing 8766 Main tab replaced its stale Drop-only document with the current campaign-and-Drop engagement section and list-state table; prior authenticated GETs show the three report pages, selectors and empty states. Campaign delivery/bounce, seed, campaign affiliate leads, DMS pivot dimensions/rates, MTD/YTD calculations, daily cache and role equivalence remain open. Tracking callback authentication now follows the AlphaLink workspace install key; synthetic coverage verifies the saved key, configured fallback and fail-closed behavior. The running-app no-op probe was skipped because no workspace with both a saved key and campaign was available. Keep DMS-DASH-001–004 TESTING.

| E-PMTA-88 | Read-only DMS `routes/mailer.php:295-297`, `PmtaController::restore_vmta/restore_vmta_v2`, `app/Console/Commands/pmta/PmtaVmta::restore/PmtaVmtaV2::restore`, and `app/Libraries/ServersInstall/Ssh::exec`; AlphaLink `PmtaConsoleController::restoreVmtaServers`, `DmsInstallerSshSession`, `PhpseclibDmsInstallerSshConnection`, `PmtaPagesTest`; source snapshot dated 2026-10-22, reviewed 2026-10-25 | DMS deletes the selected user's VMTA rows before connecting, then calls `rm -rf /etc/pmta/conf/vmta_<user-id>` and `pmta reload` without checking either command result. `Ssh::exec` returns trimmed command output or `false` when disconnected; the restore methods ignore those values and return success unless setup/command execution throws. ## E-INST-66 — Installer registrar DNS adapter — 2026-10-23

Read-only DMS source: `app/Console/Commands/servers/ServerReinstall.php:101-217`, `app/Libraries/DomainsProvider/Namecheap.php:182-203`, and `app/Libraries/DomainsProvider/Godaddy.php:140-175`. DMS sends a replacement record set to the selected registrar, uses Namecheap `namecheap.domains.dns.setHosts` parameters with SLD/TLD and MX priority 10, and for GoDaddy retains the current nameservers before replacing records, applies a 600-second minimum TTL, and assigns MX priority 10. A provider returning false is logged as `Failed Setting Records`; the DMS reinstall command continues to later stages.

AlphaLink previously bound the installation DNS boundary to a fail-closed stub. It now passes the workspace-scoped `SendingDomain` model through the stage and binds the existing `DmsRegistrarChangeDomainDnsRecordWriter`, which implements the same DMS Namecheap/GoDaddy request formats and checks the provider belongs to the domain's workspace (or is an explicitly shared default) and is active. The registrar feature tests use Laravel HTTP fakes. The related installer/domain-record/workflow suites pass **20 tests / 117 assertions**; PHPUnit reports 9 existing notices about mock objects with no expectations. `git diff --check` passes. No live registrar API call, installation, reinstallation, or DMS mutation was made. This verifies local adapter wiring and source-shaped requests, not registrar credentials or live DNS results; the full reinstall runner is still absent.

## E-SRV-LIVE-10 — DMS Change Domain review and AlphaLink LOAD — 2026-10-23

Read-only DMS source snapshot: `routes/admin.php:228-233`; `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ServerChangeDomainController.php:20-105`; `app/Actions/DomainAction.php:99-125`; `resources/views/admin/servers_change_domain/index.blade.php:1-220`; `app/Console/Commands/servers/ServerChangeDomain.php:50-115,127-154,156-298`; `app/Libraries/DomainsProvider/Namecheap.php:182-200`; `app/Libraries/DomainsProvider/Godaddy.php:140-170`. DMS offers server selection, LOAD, saved SPF/DKIM/DMARC options, HTTPS off by default, per-IP main/subdomain/domain values, group propagation, unique selected-domain options, CHANGE, and logs. The command configures the hostname and Apache vhost, applies owned-domain DNS records using saved tracking/IMAP and selected SPF/DKIM/DMARC settings, rebuilds VMTAs, optionally runs HTTPS, then saves IP/domain assignments, domain statuses and install options. A false `setHosts()` result is logged but does not abort; caught exceptions can follow earlier remote writes, and no rollback is present in the source path. The exact DMS page was not fetched because it serializes the production server and domain choice lists, which the user excluded from data-list access.

AlphaLink adds `DmsChangeDomainSelectionLoader`, the workspace-scoped `POST .../change-domain/load-ips` route, the DMS-block form and `DmsChangeDomainTest`. The focused suite passes **7 tests / 99 assertions**. On the actual AlphaLink app at `127.0.0.1:8766`, LOAD returned the saved installed server's three persisted IP rows and displayed its four option switches; an unsaved domain choice propagated to all three rows in the same original-domain group, and selecting a new MAIN IP cleared the prior MAIN. After the cached views were cleared, a fresh 8766 run confirmed the current page loads the saved assignments and enables CHANGE after LOAD. The stale compiled Blade view had continued showing the old disabled warning until `php artisan view:clear`; that cache was cleared, and a regression assertion rejects the stale copy. DmsChangeDomainRunner and the registrar writer are implemented. A synthetic-transport runner integration test now verifies the owned-domain A/MX/SPF/DMARC payload and order, VHost/VMTA/HTTPS sequence, local assignment/status/options persistence, and DMS behavior when `setHosts()` returns false; that false response is logged and later stages continue. Provider HTTP request shape is separately fake-tested. These tests do not substitute for a live host/registrar run. No CHANGE submission, real SSH command, DNS write or DMS request occurred. The browser difference is native domain select versus DMS Select2. Real host/registrar execution and same-viewport rendered-DMS comparison remain unverified; status is TESTING, not accepted.

## E-SRV-LIVE-21 — Change Domain preserves HTTPS across LOAD — 2026-10-23

Read-only DMS source: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ServerChangeDomainController.php:61-65` returns `spf`, `dkim`, `dmarc` and `data` without `https`; `resources/views/admin/servers_change_domain/index.blade.php:110-114` refreshes only SPF/DKIM/DMARC in `draw()`, leaving the current HTTPS checkbox value intact across LOADs. AlphaLink `change-domain.blade.php` now mirrors that behavior by applying only those three response values. A live browser test on `127.0.0.1:8766` loaded the saved installed `TEST_SERVER_ANIA` and its three IP rows, enabled HTTPS, then loaded the saved assignments again; HTTPS stayed enabled while the saved SPF/DKIM/DMARC values and assignments refreshed. ## E-SRV-LIVE-11 — DMS Reinstall config and IP rediscovery — 2026-10-23

Read-only DMS source: `routes/admin.php:235-240`; `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ServerReinstallController.php:21-131`; `app/Actions/ServerAction.php:103-128`; `app/Libraries/ServersInstall/Centos9.php:16-60`; `resources/views/admin/servers_reinstall/index.blade.php`; `app/Console/Commands/servers/ServerReinstall.php:45-296`. DMS LOAD CONFIG returns saved PMTA version, component switches and IP/domain assignments. RELOAD IPS calls the host's global-IP discovery, optionally removes IPv6 addresses, moves the registered primary IP to the front, keeps previous values for matching IPs, and assigns newly discovered addresses across ordered unique saved domain groups. CONFIG IPS changes the host interface configuration and may restart networking. REINSTALL reconfigures host/services, selected tracking/DNS/PMTA/VMTA/proxy/IMAP/HTTPS, firewall and local IP/domain records. The source command has no rollback for earlier effects when a later step fails.

AlphaLink adds the workspace-scoped config and read-only IP reload routes, `DmsReinstallSelectionLoader`, the form and `DmsServerReinstallTest`. The focused suite passes **6 tests / 49 assertions**. The real 8766 page loaded the saved server's PMTA 4.5 settings and three IP rows. RELOAD IPS used the encrypted saved SSH credential and internally pinned host identity; it returned three rows with one MAIN assignment and left saved options/configuration unchanged. No fingerprint prompt, server mutation or DMS request occurred. CONFIG IPS and REINSTALL were not invoked and remain disabled because the mutating network config and multi-domain DNS/host/PMTA/completion runner are not implemented. The live DMS page was not loaded because its HTML embeds production server/domain choices excluded by the user. This is partial workflow evidence, not parity acceptance.

## E-SRV-LIVE-16 — AlphaLink single Install Load IPs fix and live DMS comparison — 2026-10-23

Read-only DMS source: `/tmp/dms-rea-refresh-20261022/source/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ServersInstallController.php:26-73`, `/tmp/dms-rea-refresh-20261022/source/app/Actions/ServerAction.php:109-128`, and `/tmp/dms-rea-refresh-20261022/source/app/Libraries/ServersInstall/Centos9.php:16-38`. DMS discovers `ip -o addr show scope global`, sorts/deduplicates IPv4 before IPv6, filters IPv6 when requested, and moves the registered primary IP first only if it is present. It marks MAIN by exact equality with the registered server IP; when no main IP is found, INSTALL uses the first row's domain.

AlphaLink fix: the 8766 single Install page previously offered an unverified saved host even though its discovery transport required internal host preflight. The `loadSingleInstallIps` route now checks manager role and server eligibility before making an SSH connection, then silently runs the read-only first-host preflight and saves host identity before IP discovery. The user is not asked to provide a fingerprint. The frontend now displays a nonempty fallback for empty backend error responses, and the module URL changes with the asset modification time. `DmsSingleInstallTest` covers the preflight-before-discovery path. `node --test apps/api/tests/js/single-install.test.js` passed **2/2**; container `php artisan test --filter=DmsSingleInstallTest` passed **9 tests / 64 assertions**.

Live AlphaLink browser verification: after selecting the saved active uninstalled server and a domain, LOAD IPS rendered a row for `10.2.3.39` (IPv4) assigned to the selected domain. The server's registered provider IP `57.129.92.64` was not present in the host-reported global-scope list, so MAIN remained false, consistent with the source rule. The live host had already passed AlphaLink's read-only preflight in an earlier authorized check; therefore the browser run verifies the real address discovery and page rendering, while the new auto-preflight branch is covered by the feature test rather than a fresh unverified-host browser scenario. CONFIG IPS and INSTALL were not submitted; no remote network or installation changes occurred. The feature remains TESTING; full install outcome/log/completion parity is open.

## E-CAMP-29 — Reserve a test-send attempt before SMTP connect — 2026-10-24 10:38 UTC

Review found AlphaLink previously started the SMTP connection before storing the attempt. Two concurrent requests could therefore pass both idempotency and uncertain-result checks before either request reserved a send. The route now locks the draft campaign, rechecks that the selected revision is still latest, reserves the unique idempotency key and records `preparing` before opening the SMTP connection; duplicate keys return the stored result and do not reconnect or resend, even if SMTP settings later change. A feature test observes the persisted `preparing` attempt and start event from the SMTP `start()` boundary, then verifies the final accepted state. `CampaignTestEmailFlowTest`: 14 passed / 138 assertions; targeted Pint and `git diff --check` pass. The authenticated 8766 browser still shows the `Welcome series` demo sender `hello@example.test`, the prior no-email handshake at 10:16 UTC and zero test attempts. No email was sent. The DMS native manual test flow remains different; the lock/idempotency behavior is AlphaLink send-path hardening, not parity evidence. A user-controlled verified From domain and one explicitly authorized recipient inbox are still required.

## E-INST-86 — Production Single/Bulk install worker startup diagnosis — 2026-10-28

**Evidence source:** Read-only SSH inspection of the live AlphaLink application host, its Nginx access log, run transcript/status files and process table; `DmsInfrastructureRunLauncher`; `DmsInfrastructureRunLauncherTest`; 2026-10-28.

The production Single Install POST at 18:48:46 UTC and Bulk Install POST at 18:55:22 UTC both returned HTTP 202. Both run records remained `queued` with empty logs and no `infrastructure:execute-run` process at inspection time. PHP-FPM executes `/usr/sbin/php-fpm8.5`, while the CLI is `/usr/bin/php`; the detached launcher used `PHP_BINARY`, so it invoked the FPM executable as though it were the CLI and exited before Laravel updated run status. Running Artisan list as `www-data` succeeds, ruling out the earlier vendor-read-permission failure on this release. The launcher now selects the executable under `PHP_BINDIR` and checks it is executable. PHP syntax and a local CLI-path executable smoke check pass; the focused PHPUnit test could not run because this workspace lacks `ext-bcmath`, `ext-dom`, and `ext-xml` and has no installed `vendor/` tree. No target-server SSH/install command ran; the two observed records remain queued until separately reconciled, and production deployment plus a controlled live run are pending. Keep DMS-INST-004/005 TESTING.
